-- Lean-style type-theory scaffold.
-- This is a notation-oriented proof packet, not executable Lean.
-- theorem under test: Equivalence and coherent transport theorem for MCSP witness family
-- route synopsis: Move from `st.def.hardness-magnification-phenomenon-programme-leve` on problem `MCSP` in model `nonuniform-circuits` using `A barely-nontrivial lower bound for an explicit problem Q in a weak model` toward `st.thm.oliveira-pich-santhanam-magnification-near-state` in model `nonuniform-circuits` to obtain `major consequences such as NP not in P/poly`.
-- preserved invariant: MCSP complexity profile in nonuniform-circuits
-- route: st.def.hardness-magnification-phenomenon-programme-leve -> st.thm.oliveira-pich-santhanam-magnification-near-state
-- admissibility conditions:
--   - Recast the blocked frontier as an identity, equivalence, transport, or coherence problem rather than as a missing brute-force bridge.
constant MCSPWitnessFamily : Type
constant Admissible : MCSPWitnessFamily -> Prop
constant RouteObj : MCSPWitnessFamily -> Type
constant TargetObj : MCSPWitnessFamily -> Type
-- BridgeWitness W should be read as an equivalence witness between the route-side and target-side operationalizations.
constant BridgeWitness : MCSPWitnessFamily -> Type
constant PresInv : (W : MCSPWitnessFamily) -> BridgeWitness W -> Prop
constant RealizesTarget : (W : MCSPWitnessFamily) -> BridgeWitness W -> Prop

theorem equivalence_and_coherent_transport_theorem_for_mcsp_witness_family
    (W : MCSPWitnessFamily)
    (hW : Admissible W) :
    exists w : BridgeWitness W, PresInv W w /\ RealizesTarget W w

theorem equivalence_and_coherent_transport_theorem_for_mcsp_witness_family
    (W : MCSPWitnessFamily)
    (hW : Admissible W) :
    exists w : BridgeWitness W, PresInv W w

-- barrier variant: no_equivalence_witness
theorem equivalence_and_coherent_transport_theorem_for_mcsp_witness_family_barrier
    (W : MCSPWitnessFamily)
    (hW : Admissible W) :
    not (exists w : BridgeWitness W, PresInv W w /\ RealizesTarget W w)
```

-- This is a notation-oriented proof packet, not executable Lean.
-- theorem under test: Transport theorem for MCSP witness family
-- route synopsis: Move from `st.def.hardness-magnification-phenomenon-programme-leve` in `nonuniform-circuits` toward `st.thm.atserias-m-uller-simple-general-magnification-fr` in `unspecified`.
-- preserved invariant: exponential-lower-bound witness complexity
-- route: st.def.hardness-magnification-phenomenon-programme-leve -> st.thm.atserias-m-uller-simple-general-magnification-fr
-- admissibility conditions:

constant Admissible : MCSPWitnessFamily -> Prop
constant RouteObj : MCSPWitnessFamily -> Type
constant TargetObj : MCSPWitnessFamily -> Type

-- Lean-style type-theory scaffold.
-- This is a notation-oriented proof packet, not executable Lean.
-- theorem under test: Obstruction theorem for MCSP witness family
-- route synopsis: Move from `st.def.hardness-magnification-phenomenon-programme-leve` in `nonuniform-circuits` toward `st.thm.oliveira-pich-santhanam-magnification-near-state` in `nonuniform-circuits`.
-- preserved invariant: MCSP complexity profile in nonuniform-circuits
-- route: st.def.hardness-magnification-phenomenon-programme-leve -> st.thm.oliveira-pich-santhanam-magnification-near-state
-- admissibility conditions:
--   - `MCSP witness family` is explicitly defined in the current route.
constant MCSPWitnessFamily : Type
constant Admissible : MCSPWitnessFamily -> Prop
constant RouteObj : MCSPWitnessFamily -> Type
constant TargetObj : MCSPWitnessFamily -> Type
-- BridgeWitness W should be read as a would-be witness whose nonexistence would certify the barrier.
constant BridgeWitness : MCSPWitnessFamily -> Type
constant PresInv : (W : MCSPWitnessFamily) -> BridgeWitness W -> Prop
constant RealizesTarget : (W : MCSPWitnessFamily) -> BridgeWitness W -> Prop

    not (exists w : BridgeWitness W, PresInv W w /\ RealizesTarget W w)
